## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- mcp-lifecycle-module-operator (also known as\: MCP Lifecycle Module operator, MCP Lifecycle operator) is a Kubernetes component.

- deploying 5 workload(s) (controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager) with 5 container(s).
- watching 11 resource type(s).
- exposing 1 service(s) on ports\: 443.
- defining 1 API spec type(s) across 8 struct(s) with 23 total fields.
